# Metzudat David on I Samuel

מצודת דוד על שמואל א

Link: [https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel)

פירוש קלאסי מן המאה ה-18 על נביאים וכתובים, המתבסס בעיקר על פירוש רד"ק ומבאר את הפסוקים בשפה ברורה ופשוטה.

18th-century classic commentary on Prophets and Writings explaining verses in clear and simple language, primarily based on the Radak’s commentary.

Sections: Chapter-Verse-Comment

Location: Tanakh/Acharonim on Tanakh/Metzudat David/Prophets

Originally from [Sefaria](https://www.sefaria.org) via [TorahLM.org](https://torahlm.org) — see [licensing](https://torahlm.org/sefaria_license).

Version (He): On Your Way (Segments: 1213, License: Public Domain)

Version (En): Sefaria Community Translation (Segments: 97)


## Chapter 1


### Verse 1

###### Metzudat David on I Samuel 1:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:1:1)


**מן הרמתים צופים.** הוא שם מקום, על שם שהיו בה שתי גבעות רמות, צופים ומקבילים זה מול זה:


**Ramatayim of the Tzufites:** That is the name of the place, due to there being two tall (*ramot*) mountains, viewing (*tzofot*) one across from the other.


###### Metzudat David on I Samuel 1:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:1:2)


**אפרתי.** על צוף יאמר, שהיה גם הוא מהר אפרים, ולא מבני אפרים, כי לוי היה ומבני קרח:


**Efrati (Ephraimite):** It is speaking about Tzuf. As he too was from Mount Ephraim, but not from the Children of Ephraim. From he was a Levite, specifically from the sons of Korach.



### Verse 3

###### Metzudat David on I Samuel 1:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:3:1)


**ועלה האיש ההוא.** מעולם היה מנהגו לעלות אל שילה בכל שנה ושנה, להשתחות ולזבוח לה׳:


**This man used to go up:** His custom was always to go up to Shiloh every year, to bow down and sacrifice to God.


###### Metzudat David on I Samuel 1:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:3:2)


**כהנים.** רוצה לומר: סגני הכהונה, כי עלי אביהם שימש בכהונה גדולה:


**Priests:** Meaning to say, assistants of the [high] priesthood. As their father, Eli, served as the high priest.



### Verse 4

###### Metzudat David on I Samuel 1:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:4:1)


**ויהי היום.** רוצה לומר ; וכאשר בא היום של הבאת קרבנותיו, היה אלקנה זובח שלמים, והיה נותן לפנינה וכו׳ מנות מזבח השלמים:


**And it was on that day:** Meaning to say, when the day came that he would bring his sacrifices, Elkanah would sacrifice peace-offerings and he would give portions to Peninah, etc. from the sacrifice of the peace-offerings.



### Verse 5

###### Metzudat David on I Samuel 1:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:5:1)


**מנה אחת.** רוצה לומר: מיוחדת ביופי, ולתוספת ביאור אמר ׳אפים׳, רוצה לומר: הראויה לקבל אותה בסבר פנים יפות: וה׳ סגר רחמה, ולא ילדה, והיתה מצירה ולזה הראה לה חיבה יתירה להפיג צערה:


**One portion:** Meaning to say, unique in its fineness. And to explain further, it said, "*apayim* (face)" - one fitting to be received with a joyful face.



### Verse 6

###### Metzudat David on I Samuel 1:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:6:1)


**וכעסתה צרתה.** פנינה צרתה, היתה מכעסת אותה, בדברים:


**And her rival would anger her:** Peninah, her rival wife, would anger her with words.


###### Metzudat David on I Samuel 1:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:6:2)


**גם כעס.** רוצה לומר: וחוזרת ומכעסת פעמים רבות:


**Very much anger:** Meaning to say, she would repeat it and anger [her] often.


###### Metzudat David on I Samuel 1:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:6:3)


**בעבור הרעימה.** בכדי שתהיה חנה זעופה וקצופה:


**So that she should complain:** In order that Channah would be furious and fume.


###### Metzudat David on I Samuel 1:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:6:4)


**כי ה׳ סגר רחמה.** רוצה לומר: ובזה היה מקום לפנינה להכעיסה, וכמו שאמרו רבותינו ז״ל (ילקוט שמעוני רמז עז): שאמרה לה בדרך לעג, כלום קנית מעפורת לבנך הגדול, או חלוק לבנך הקטן והיא גם היא נפשה מרה לה בעבור עקרותה, ונוח הדבר להכעיסה:


**Because the Lord had shut up her womb:** Meaning to say, and there was room for Peninah to anger her with this. And it is like our Rabbis, may their memory be blessed, said, "She said to her mockingly, 'Did you buy your older son a cloak today, or your younger son, a shirt?'" And she was bitter-spirited as well on account of her barrenness, and the matter would easily anger her.



### Verse 7

###### Metzudat David on I Samuel 1:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:7:1)


**וכן יעשה.** כן עשה אלקנה בכל שנה, לתת לחנה מנה אחת אפים:


**And as he would do this:** Elkanah would do this each year, to give Channah a choice portion.


###### Metzudat David on I Samuel 1:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:7:2)


**כן תכעיסנה.** כמו וכן תכעיסנה, ורוצה לומר: הוא עשה את שלו להראות לה חיבה, וכן פנינה עשתה את שלה:


**So she would anger her:** Like, so she would anger her (the spelling of the word could also be read as, "So would she get angry.") And it is meaning to say, he would do his [part] to show love to her, and Peninah would likewise do her [part, to anger her].


###### Metzudat David on I Samuel 1:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:7:3)


**ותבכה.** בכל עת בואה אל שילה, בכתה מרוב התוגה, ובעבור זה נמנע ממנה תאות האכילה:


**And she wept:** Every time they would come to Shiloh, she would cry from the great grief. And because of that, she was prevented from having the desire to eat.



### Verse 8

###### Metzudat David on I Samuel 1:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:8:1)


**ולמה ירע לבבך.** למה יהיה לבבך נשבר מתוגיון חסרון בנים:


**And why is your heart grieved:** Why should your heart be broken from grieving for the lack of children?


###### Metzudat David on I Samuel 1:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:8:2)


**הלא אנכי.** תרגם יונתן: **הלא רעותי.** רצה לומר: הלא רצוני אליך ואהבתי לך, טובה היא לך מאלו ילדת עשרה בנים:


**Am I not:** Yonatan [ben Uzziel] translated [it as], "Is not my wish" - meaning to say, "Is not my wish towards you and my love for you better to you than if you had given birth to ten sons?"



### Verse 9

###### Metzudat David on I Samuel 1:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:9:1)


**ותקם חנה.** ללכת אל בית ה׳:


**Channah rose** to go to the House of the Lord.


###### Metzudat David on I Samuel 1:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:9:2)


**אחרי אכלה.** עם שנאמר למעלה ותבכה ולא תאכל, מכל מקום אכלה מעט להחיות את הנפש:


**After eating:** Even if it is stated above (I Samuel 1:7), "and she wept and would not eat," she nevertheless ate a little to keep herself alive.



### Verse 10

###### Metzudat David on I Samuel 1:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:10:1)


**והיא מרת נפש.** בבואה להתפלל, היתה נפשה מרה לה במאוד:


**And she was bitter in spirit:** When she came to pray, her spirit was very bitter.



### Verse 11

###### Metzudat David on I Samuel 1:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:11:1)


**בעני.** במה שאני מעונה בצער ותוגיון:


**Upon the affliction:** Upon that which I am afflicted with pain and grief.


###### Metzudat David on I Samuel 1:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:11:2)


**וזכרתני ולא תשכח.** כפל הדבר במלות שונות לתוספת ביאור, וכדרך המתפללים:


**And You will remember me and not forget:** It repeated the thing with different words for further clarification and in the way of those praying.


###### Metzudat David on I Samuel 1:11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:11:3)


**זרע אנשים.** רוצה לומר: בנים זכרים:


**The offspring of men:** Meaning to say, male children.


###### Metzudat David on I Samuel 1:11:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:11:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:11:4)


**ונתתיו לה׳.** רוצה לומר: אקבל עלי בנדר, שאתן אותו לה׳:


**Then I will give him to the Lord:** Meaning to say, I will take it upon myself as a vow that I will give him to the Lord.


###### Metzudat David on I Samuel 1:11:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:11:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:11:5)


**כל ימי חייו.** רוצה לומר: תמיד יתבודד בעבודת ה׳:


**All the days of his life:** Meaning to say, he will always dedicate himself to the service of the Lord.


###### Metzudat David on I Samuel 1:11:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:11:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:11:6)


**ומורה וכו׳.** יהיה נזיר עד עולם, לבל יתגלח וכדין הנזיר:


**And a razor, etc.:** He will be a nazerite forever without shaving, and like the law of a nazerite.



### Verse 12

###### Metzudat David on I Samuel 1:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:12:1)


**שומר את פיה.** רוצה לומר: היה מסתכל לשמור מוצא שפתיה, לשמוע מדבריה:


**Watched her mouth:** Meaning to say, he would watch what came out of her lips, to hear her words.



### Verse 13

###### Metzudat David on I Samuel 1:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:13:1)


**וחנה.** אבל חנה היתה מדברת על לבה, רוצה לומר: בלחש, כאלו מדברת אל לבה, ולא היה נשמע לעלי:


**And Channah:** But, "Channah was praying in her heart" - meaning to say, in a whisper, as if she was speaking to her heart, so it was not audible to Eli.


###### Metzudat David on I Samuel 1:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:13:2)


**ויחשבה עלי לשכורה.** כי כן דרך השכור לנענע בשפתיו, ואין אומר ואין דברים:


**And he thought she was drunk:** For this is the way of a drunkard to move his lips, but there is 'no speech and no words.'



### Verse 14

###### Metzudat David on I Samuel 1:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:14:1)


**תשתכרין.** תראי בעצמך את השכרות זה זמן מרובה, ועד מתי:


**Will you be drunk:** Will you show yourself to be drunk a long time; and until when?


###### Metzudat David on I Samuel 1:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:14:2)


**הסירי את יינך.** אם שתית יין הרבה, עסקי להסיר שכרות היין בשינה וכדומה:


**Remove your wine:** If you drank much wine, do something to remove the drunkenness of the wine, through sleep and that which is similar.



### Verse 15

###### Metzudat David on I Samuel 1:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:15:1)


**לא אדני.** רוצה לומר: אתה אדוני, לא כן הדבר, אבל אני אשה קשת רוח, רוצה לומר: מצרה ודאגה, ומדעת באתי הנה להתפלל, ולא מבלי דעת כשכור, כי יין ושכר לא שתיתי:


**No, my lord:** Meaning to say - you, my lord, the matter is not like this. Rather, "I am a woman of troubled spirit," meaning to say, distressed and worried. But I have come here knowingly to pray; and not without intention like a drunkard. For "I have drunk neither wine nor strong drink."


###### Metzudat David on I Samuel 1:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:15:2)


**ואשפוך וכו׳.** רוצה לומר: ואל תתמה על מה זה שפתי נעות וקולי לא ישמע, כי הלא לפני ה׳ אשפוך נפשי, ומה לי להרים קול להשמיע לזולת:


**But I have been pouring out, etc.:** Meaning to say, and do not wonder about that which my lips move but my voice is not heard. For is it not that it is before the Lord that I have been pouring out my soul? So why should I raise my voice to make it heard to another?



### Verse 16

###### Metzudat David on I Samuel 1:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:16:1)


**אל תתן.** אל תחשבני לבת בליעל לבוא שכורה לבית ה׳, כלומר, אם האמת כדבריך, מה לי להאריך בתפלה כל כך:


**Do not take:** Do not think of me as an unbridled woman, to come drunk to the House of the Lord. Meaning to say, if the truth is like your words, why should I prolong my words so much in prayer?


###### Metzudat David on I Samuel 1:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:16:2)


**כי מרוב שיכי וכעסי.** כי בעבור רוב ספור התלאות והכעס אשר פנינה מכעסת אותי, דברתי לפני ה׳ עד הנה, היות רבים הם:


**For it is out of the greatness of my grievance and my anger:** For it is due to the greatness of the telling of the troubles and the anger with which Peninah angered me that I spoke in front of the Lord until now, as they are great.



### Verse 17

###### Metzudat David on I Samuel 1:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:17:1)


**יתן את שלתך.** ימלא את בקשתך, ובדרך תפלה אמר:


**He grant what you have asked:** Fulfill your request. And he said [this] in the way of a prayer.



### Verse 18

###### Metzudat David on I Samuel 1:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:18:1)


**תמצא.** רוצה לומר: הנה מצאתי חן בעיניך, כי מה שבקשת עלי רחמים, יחשב לי למציאות חן, והחזיקה לו טובה בדבריה:


**May she find:** Meaning to say, behold I have found favor in your eyes. For I consider that which you requested mercy upon me, to be finding favor. So she showed thankfulness with her words.


###### Metzudat David on I Samuel 1:18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:18:2)


**ותאכל.** מעתה אכלה כל צרכה, ולא היה לה עוד הזעם שהיה לה מאז, כי בטחה בתפלת עלי:


**And she ate:** From now on, she ate all she needed and she no longer had the fury that she had for a long time. For she trusted in Eli's prayer.



### Verse 19

###### Metzudat David on I Samuel 1:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:19:1)


**ויזכרה ה׳.** עלתה זכרונה לפניו לטובה:


**And the Lord remembered her:** Her recollection came up before Him for the good.



### Verse 20

###### Metzudat David on I Samuel 1:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:20:1)


**לתקופות הימים ותהר חנה.** רוצה לומר: לעת הקיף תשלום ימי ההריון, מעת אשר הרתה חנה:


**As the cycles and days Channah conceived passed:** Meaning to say, at the time of the completion of the cycle of days of the pregnancy from the time that Channah conceived.


###### Metzudat David on I Samuel 1:20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:20:2)


**ותלד בן.** בתשלום ההיקף:


**And she gave birth to a son,** at the end of the cycle.


###### Metzudat David on I Samuel 1:20:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:20:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:20:3)


**כי מה׳ שאלתיו.** והוא לשון נופל על הלשון:


**For I requested him from the Lord:** And it is a play on words.



### Verse 21

###### Metzudat David on I Samuel 1:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:21:1)


**ויעל.** כשבא עת עליתו, עלה לזבוח:


**Went up:** When the time of his going up arrived, he went up to sacrifice.


###### Metzudat David on I Samuel 1:21:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:21:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:21:2)


**את זבח הימים.** מה שהיה רגיל לזבוח בכל שנה:


**The annual sacrifice:** What he was accustomed to sacrificing every year.


###### Metzudat David on I Samuel 1:21:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:21:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:21:3)


**ואת נדרו.** מה שנדר כל השנה והוא בביתו, הביאם לזבוח בעת עלותו:


**And his vow:** He brought that which he vowed the whole year and was in his house, to sacrifice them at the time of his going up.



### Verse 22

###### Metzudat David on I Samuel 1:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:22:1)


**לא עלתה.** בהשנה ההיא:


**Did not go up:** That year.


###### Metzudat David on I Samuel 1:22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:22:2)


**עד יגמל הנער.** רוצה לומר: לא אזוז מביתי עד יגמל הנער, ואחרי הגמלו, אביא גם אותו אל בית ה׳, ונראה את פני ה׳ להשתחוות לפניו ולהודות לו על הנער היולד:


**Until the boy will be weaned:** Meaning to say, I will not move from here "until the boy will be weaned." But after he is weaned, "I will bring him" also to the House of the Lord, "that he may appear before the Lord," to bow down before Him; and to thank Him for the boy that was born.


###### Metzudat David on I Samuel 1:22:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:22:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:22:3)


**וישב שם.** הנער ישב שם עד עולם, רוצה לומר: כל ימי עלי, בכדי לשמש אותו:


**And he shall abide there:** The boy will abide there forever - meaning to say, all the days of Eli, so that he may serve him.



### Verse 23

###### Metzudat David on I Samuel 1:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:23:1)


**הטוב בעיניך.** רוצה לומר: כפי חפצך עשי, ושבי עד גמלך אותו:


**What is proper in your eyes:** Meaning to say, do according to your wish, and remain until you wean him.


###### Metzudat David on I Samuel 1:23:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:23:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:23:2)


**אך יקם ה׳ את דברו.** רצה לומר: מה שאמרת פה שתשבי עד גמלך, הנה הדבר הזה מסור בידך לעשות בעצמך כאשר תרצי, אך אשר דברת על הנער, שהוא ישב בבית ה׳ עד עולם, הנה דבר זה אין בידך להדירו, אך ה׳ יקם את הדבר אשר דברת על הנער, והוא יתן בלבו לשבת בביתו עד עולם (ומלת דברו מוסב על הנער, רצה לומר: דבר הנאמר בו ועליו):


**But may the Lord fulfill His/his word:** Meaning to say, that which you said here that you will remain until you wean [him], behold this thing is given over to your hands, to do with yourself as you want. But that which you said about the boy, that he should abide in the House of the Lord forever, behold that thing is not in your hands to vow it. However, "May the Lord fulfill" the word that you said about the boy, and He place it into his heart to abide in His house forever. (And the word, "his word," is referring to the boy, meaning to say, the word that was said about the boy and regarding him.)


###### Metzudat David on I Samuel 1:23:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:23:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:23:3)


**ותשב האשה.** בביתה, ולא עלתה:


**So the woman remained** in her home and did not go up.



### Verse 24

###### Metzudat David on I Samuel 1:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:24:1)


**בפרים שלשה.** עם שלשה פרים לזבוח לה׳, והקמח למנחות, והיין לנסכים:


**With three bulls:** With three bulls to sacrifice to the Lord, and the flour for the grain offering and the wine for the libations.


###### Metzudat David on I Samuel 1:24:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:24:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:24:2)


**ותביאהו.** את הנער:


**And she brought him:** The lad.


###### Metzudat David on I Samuel 1:24:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:24:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:24:3)


**והנער נער.** רצה לומר: הנער היה עודנו נער קטן ורך, ועם כל זה לא נמנעה מלהביאו כאשר גמלתו:


**And the lad was a lad:** Meaning to say, the lad was a small and delicate lad. Yet, in spite of all this, it did not prevent her from bringing him when she weaned him.



### Verse 25

###### Metzudat David on I Samuel 1:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:25:1)


**וישחטו וכו׳.** רוצה לומר: כאשר שחטו הפר הראשון, הביאו את הנער אל עלי, להראותו שנתקבלה תפלתו:


**And they slaughtered, etc.:** Meaning to say, when they slaughtered the first bull, they brought the lad to Eli to show him that his prayer was accepted.



### Verse 26

###### Metzudat David on I Samuel 1:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:26:1)


**בי אדני.** בקשה מעלי והשביעתו בחיי נפשו, לשום עינו על הנער:


**Please, my lord:** She requested from Eli, and made him swear by his own soul, to keep his eye on the lad.


###### Metzudat David on I Samuel 1:26:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:26:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:26:2)


**הנצבת.** אשר עמדתי פה בהיותך גם אתה יושב פה, כמו שכתוב למעלה: ועלי הכהן יושב וגו׳:


**Who stood:** Who stood here when you too were here sitting, as it is written above (I Samuel 1:9), "and Eli the priest sat, etc."



### Verse 27

###### Metzudat David on I Samuel 1:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:27:1)


**אל הנער הזה התפללתי.** רוצה לומר: התפלה שהתפללתי אז, היתה לשאלת בן, והנה זהו אשר ילדתי:


**It was this boy I prayed for:** Meaning to say, the prayer that I had prayed then was to ask for a son. And behold, this is the one to whom I gave birth.



### Verse 28

###### Metzudat David on I Samuel 1:28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:28:1)


**וגם וכו׳.** רוצה לומר: גם בעת התפלה נדרתי אז להשאילו לה׳, להתבודד תמיד בעבודתו:


**And also:** Meaning to say, also at the time of the prayer, I then vowed to lend him to the Lord, to always dedicate himself to His service.


###### Metzudat David on I Samuel 1:28:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:28:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:28:2)


**אשר היה.** כמו אשר יהיה, רוצה לומר: כל זמן אשר יהיה בזה העולם, הוא שאול לה׳:


**That he was (lived):** It is like, as he will be - meaning to say, all the time that he will be in this world, he is lent to the Lord.


###### Metzudat David on I Samuel 1:28:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:28:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:28:3)


**וישתחו שם לה׳.** השתחוה להודות לה׳, אשר שם חלקו מעובדי ה׳:


**And he bowed down to the Lord there:** He bowed down to thank the Lord, who set up his portion to be among the servants of the Lord.



## Chapter 2


### Verse 1

###### Metzudat David on I Samuel 2:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:1:1)


**ותתפלל חנה.** עצם התפלה היא בסוף אמריה, ובתחלה סדרה שבחו של מקום, ולזה נאמר ותאמר:


**And Channah prayed:** The actual prayer is at the end of her words; but, at the beginning, she structured the praises of the Omnipresent. And that is why it states, "and she said" [other words that introduced the prayer].


###### Metzudat David on I Samuel 2:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:1:2)


**עלץ לבי בה׳.** בתשועת ה׳ שמח לבי:


**My heart exults in the Lord:** My heart rejoices in the salvation of the Lord.


###### Metzudat David on I Samuel 2:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:1:3)


**רמה קרני.** לנגח בו אויבי, ועל פנינה ובניה אמרה, והוא ענין מליצה, לומר שמעתה לא יוכלו לצער אותה כמאז:


**My horn is exalted:** To gore my enemies; and she said this about Peninah and her sons. And it is a type of metaphor to say that from now on, they would not be able to pain her like before.


###### Metzudat David on I Samuel 2:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:1:4)


**רחב פי.** כי עד הנה הייתי כשה נאלמה לא תפתח פיה, ועתה רחב פי על אויבי, להשיב אמרים:


**My mouth is enlarged:** Since, until now, I was like a silent lamb which does not open its mouth. But now, my mouth is enlarged upon my enemies, to answer with words.


###### Metzudat David on I Samuel 2:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:1:5)


**כי שמחתי בישועתך.** שמחתי היא על אשר בא התשועה ממך, ויש די בזה להשיב אמרים:


**Because I rejoice in Your salvation:** My joy is about that which the salvation comes from You. And there is enough in this to answer with words.



### Verse 2

###### Metzudat David on I Samuel 2:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:2:1)


**כי אין בלתך.** רוצה לומר: אין בורא בלתך, ומי מהנבראים אשר ידמה קדושתו לקדושת הבורא:


**For there is none beside You:** Meaning to say, there is no creator besides You; and who among the creatures has holiness comparable to the holiness of the Creator?



### Verse 3

###### Metzudat David on I Samuel 2:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:3:1)


**אל תרבו וכו׳.** אמרה נגד פנינה ובניה, אל תרבו לדבר דבר גבוהה, רוצה לומר: גאוה וגסות, וכפלה המלה, להורות על גאוה גדולה והתמדתה:


**Do not abound, etc.:** She said it about Peninah and her sons, do not abound in speaking uppity things - meaning to say, pride and rudeness. And she repeated the word (elevated), to indicate great pride and its constancy.


###### Metzudat David on I Samuel 2:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:3:2)


**יצא עתק.** ׳אל׳ שאמרה, משמשת במקום שנים, כאלו אמרה אל יצא עתק מפיכם:


**Arrogance come out:** The [one], "do not," that she said, serves in the place of two. ("Do not" only actually appears at the beginning of this verse, and not before this phrase). It is as if she said, "Do not let arrogance come out of your mouths."


###### Metzudat David on I Samuel 2:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:3:3)


**כי אל דעות ה׳.** ה׳ הוא אל היודע הכל, ולפניו נמנים מעשה הבריות:


**For the Lord is a God of knowledge:** The Lord is a God who knows everything, and the acts of the creatures are enumerated before Him.



### Verse 4

###### Metzudat David on I Samuel 2:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:4:1)


**קשת.** רוצה לומר: ועל שהוא יודע הכל, לזה משלם לאיש כמעשהו, ופתאום יושברו קשת וחוזק הגבורים, והנכשלים אזרו חיל:


**The bows:** Meaning to say, and since he knows everything - because of that, He repays a person according to his deed. So suddenly the bows and the strength of the mighty are broken, "and the faltering are girded with strength."



### Verse 5

###### Metzudat David on I Samuel 2:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:5:1)


**שבעים.** אנשים שהיו שבעים נעשו רעבים, עד שישכרו עצמם לעבודה בעבור הלחם:


**The sated:** People who were sated became hungry, to the point that they must hire themselves out to work for the sake of bread.


###### Metzudat David on I Samuel 2:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:5:2)


**ורעבים.** ואשר היו משכירים עצמם בעבור הרעבון, חדלו מלהשכיר עצמן, כי מצאו די מחסורם:


**And the hungry:** And those who hired themselves out on account of their hunger stopped hiring themselves out, as they found enough for their needs.


###### Metzudat David on I Samuel 2:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:5:3)


**עד עקרה ילדה שבעה.** רוצה לומר: כל כך רבה השגחתו, עד אשר מי שהיתה עקרה, ילדה שבעה בנים, ומי שהיתה בת רבת בנים, נכרתה באבדן כל בניה:


**Until the barren has born seven:** Meaning to say, His providence is so great, until the one who was barren has born seven sons and the one who was the mother of many is cut off by the loss of all her sons.



### Verse 6

###### Metzudat David on I Samuel 2:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:6:1)


**ה׳ ממית ומחיה.** רוצה לומר: לא במקרה בא, כי אם יד ה׳ עשתה זאת הוא ממית בני רבת הבנים, והוא מחיה בני העקרה, ולא תשכל מלידה ומבטן ומהריון:


**The Lord kills, and gives life:** Meaning to say, this does not come by happenstance, but rather the hand of the Lord did this. He kills the sons of the mother of many, and He gives life to the sons of the barren one, so [that] she does not become bereaved from birth, from the womb and from pregnancy.


###### Metzudat David on I Samuel 2:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:6:2)


**מוריד שאול.** ובזה מוריד את בת רבת הבנים אל השאול, רוצה לומר: בשפל המצב ועומק הצרה, ואת העקרה מעלה מעומק שפל מצבה:


**He brings down to the grave:** And with this, he brings the mother of many to the grave - meaning to say, with the lowliness of this condition and the depth of the distress; whereas He raises the barren one from the deep lowliness of her condition.



### Verse 7

###### Metzudat David on I Samuel 2:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:7:1)


**ה׳ מוריש.** את השבעים, עד אשר בלחם נשכרו:


**The Lord makes poor** the sated, until they are hired out for bread.


###### Metzudat David on I Samuel 2:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:7:2)


**ומעשיר.** את הרעבים, עד אשר חדלו:


**And makes rich** the hungry, until they cease [to hire themselves out].


###### Metzudat David on I Samuel 2:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:7:3)


**משפיל.** בזה משפיל גאות השבעים, ואף מרומם עליהם את הרעבים, ולא די במה שחדלו מלהשכיר עצמם, כי אף ישכרו את השבעים וירומו עוד עליהם:


**And brings low:** He brings low the pride of the [formerly] sated ones through this. And He even raises the hungry over them. So it is not enough that they cease hiring themselves out, but they also hire the sated ones, and they rise further above them.

**To make them inherit:** Meaning to say, they will be honored in the eyes of the creatures as if they were accustomed to honor that they inherited from their fathers.

**For the Lord’s:** And that is because the foundations of the world are the Lord's; and He made the world upon those foundations. That is why it is in His hands to do as He wishes in His world.



### Verse 9

###### Metzudat David on I Samuel 2:9:1

**רגלי חסידיו ישמור.** רוצה לומר: אולם הכל במשפט, כי רגלי חסידיו ישמור מלכד, והרשעים יוכרתו בבוא חושך הפורעניות, בעת נתמלא סאתם:


**He guards the steps of His faithful:** Meaning to say, indeed everything is with justice - for the steps of the righteous are guarded from being caught; but the evildoers are cut off when the darkness of [their] punishments comes, when their measure is full.


###### Metzudat David on I Samuel 2:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:9:2)


**כי לא בכח.** אין התגברות האיש תלוי בכחו, כי אם היא ביד ה׳, ועל פי כשרון מעשה האיש:


**For not by strength:** A person's surmounting is not dependent upon his strength, but it is rather in the hands of the Lord and according to the propriety of a person's deed.



### Verse 10

###### Metzudat David on I Samuel 2:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:10:1)


**ה׳ יחתו מריביו.** כחתימה לסדר שבחי המקום, חזרה להתפלל על שמואל בנה ואמרה, אתה ה׳ נא יחתו וישברו מריביו של בני ואנשי מלחמתו, כי ראתה ברוח הקדש שפלשתים ילחמו בו, והתפללה שהמה יחתו:


**O Lord, may His/his adversaries be shattered:** As a closure to the order of the praises of the Omnipresent, she returned to praying for Shmuel, her son. So she said, "You O Lord, please let the adversaries of my son and the men fighting him be shattered and broken." For she saw with the holy spirit that the Philistines would fight against him, so she prayed that they would be shattered.


###### Metzudat David on I Samuel 2:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:10:2)


**עליו בשמים ירעם.** בעבור בני, ירעם האל יתברך בשמים על פלשתים ונתקיימה תפלתה כמו שכתבו (לקמן ז י): וירעם ה׳ בקול גדול וגו׳ על פלשתים:


**Out of heaven shall/may He thunder upon them:** For the sake of my son, God - may He be blessed - should thunder over the Philistines. And her prayer was fulfilled - like it was written (I Samuel 7:10), "and the Lord thundered with a great sound, etc. against the Philistines."


###### Metzudat David on I Samuel 2:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:10:3)


**ה׳ ידין אפסי ארץ.** רוצה לומר: **אתה ה׳, נא ידין בני אפסי ארץ, להיות שופט בישראל, ויסבב במקומות עריהם לשפטם.** וכן נתקיים כמו שכתוב (שם שם טז): והלך וגו׳ וסבב וגו׳ ושפט את ישראל:


**The Lord shall judge the ends of the earth:** Meaning to say, You O Lord, please let my son judge the ends of the land, to be a judge in Israel and to go around in the places of their cities, to judge them. And this was likewise fulfilled, as it is written (I Samuel 7:16), "And he went, etc. and made the rounds, etc. and judged Israel."

**ויתן עוז למלכו.** ה׳ יתן עוז וגבורה להמלך אשר בני ימליך אותו, והוא שאול:


**And He shall give power to His/his king:** May the Lord give power and strength to the king whom my son will crown, who is Shaul.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:10:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20David%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:10:5)


**וירם קרן משיחו.** על דוד אמרה, כי שמואל בנה רק משחו למלך, אבל לא המליכו בחייו, כי שמואל מת ועודנו לא מלך עד לאחר זמן, והתפללה עליו חנה שירום קרן ממשלתו:


**And exalt the horn of His/his anointed:** She said this about David. For Shmuel, her son, only anointed him as the king, but he did not crown him in his lifetime. As when Shmuel died, he was still not king until later. And Channah prayed about him, that the horn of his governance should be exalted.
